## What is selected

This is the pinned Fudan JUMP family, not another128 grounded-drive patch. Source formula mapping:

| Upstream family |130 selected quantity/adaptation |
| --- | --- |
| tracking_lin_vel / enhance, weights1/1 | Original2exp(-error/.25) and2(exp(-error/2.5)-1), task world XY command; enhanced cost bounded by matching tracker to keep request reward nonnegative. |
| tracking_ang_vel, weight1 | Original exp(-body_yaw_rate²/.25), zero yaw-rate command. |
| encourage_jump, upward part.15*vz+ | Selected at ground/zero-ready without vx/near/air prerequisite; velocity credit caps at inherited2.4m/s. Stateful accumulator remains unavailable and unselected. |
| takeoff_extend, weight.5 | Original exp(-4sum|L-Lext|), support-history and root-vz>.15, before edge; Lext is current own FK of the saved own extension shape. No required prior crouch. |
| line_z, weight6, original cap2.5 | Positive minimum of root, BOTH wheel-centre and BOTH minimum-point world-Z velocities; current geometry/contact-history air surrogate. Fades as real wheel clearance reaches270mm. Root-only rise/spin cannot earn this main ascent term. |
| flight, weight.15 | Current air/qualified-landing witness times actual two-wheel clearance fraction; unloading alone has no flight offset. |
| base_height_flight, weight6/cap2.5 | Original exponential shape applied to BOTH actual wheel bottoms with target.27m. Subtract floor value and normalize, then scale to the original2.5 cap. Strict height gradient up to270mm; no early6*exp clipping plateau or body-height substitution. |
| leg_tuck, weight1.7 | Original exp(-4sum|L-Ltuck|), own FK tucked shape, air/qualified-landing witness and actual wheel-clearance fraction. No foreign.16m target or ground tuck jackpot. |
| JUMP action/torque/nominal/orientation/ang_xy/theta costs | Original JUMP weighted/clipped terms feed the retained bounded exp(.05*clamp_min(sum/dt,-10)) effort attenuation. This intentionally replaces former RECOVERY weights in request effort. Unavailable original terms remain explicit, not forged. |

Original task top3*.04 and quiet1*.04 bonuses remain contingent on126 qualified same-flight BOTH crosses and true top support surrogate. Quiet speed scale.03 remains; physical original top-contact12 and stable200 callbacks are independent and unchanged. Qualified touchdown keeps height/tuck credit through the partial-footprint corridor, avoiding the known moving-landing gap. Original nonrequest recovery and pre2s goal are byte-equivalent; diagnostic.31/.16 metadata remains diagnostic only.

Dense air is **not physical success**: actual own wheel-bottom geometry and nonwheel clearance, plus the existing two-control wheel-force history are available in raw127. It requires neither pre2s ready nor prior qualified flight, so the actual observed ready0 branch can receive preparation/ascent/height gradients. Strict126 readiness/single-flight/cross memory remains unchanged; an episode with frozen ready0 still cannot receive the qualified-cross/top reward.130 does not fabricate that qualification or claim to fix physical eligibility.

## Bounds and remaining limitations

Request reward is finite/nonnegative for valid finite inputs and at most.19 (declared conservative max remains.20). Tracking pair≤.02, yaw≤.01, pre-support jump families≤.0771, top+quiet≤.16: `.03+.0771*(1-P)+.16*P <= .19`. Effort≤1 and existing closure deduction≤half the nonnegative request subtotal. `combine_closure_risk`, soft termination,2mm reset and all original physical events are unchanged.

The root-up/extension preparation terms remain proxies and are not mathematically cycle-neutral. Their maximum combined credit is.0036+.005/control, materially smaller than128's.02 drive budget; real-wheel ascent/height/tuck are the main jump terms. Full synthetic jump→cross→top returns must beat retained stance/extension/microhop/shuttle examples in the new fixture. That is a finite set of algebraic comparisons, not proof against every cycle or an actuator feasibility proof.

The fixture retains delay16/40,effort.8 and asymmetric closure risks as honest reported rankings. Delay40 is deliberately a32-step identical static prefix: equal-state/equal-effort ties, higher effort costs lose, because no reward may know the future. Delay16 and risk reversals, if present, remain explicit H32/critic limitations, not hidden by a PASS label. Compression is not forcibly prescribed or paid as fictitious spring energy. Actual action→acceleration, physical useful liftoff,270mm reachability, WM/critic accuracy and eventual policy preference remain UNKNOWN.

"""Bind two saved own STATIC_KINEMATIC_FIT shapes to actual own FK lengths.

No foreign leg length, animation, timing, action, IK solve or physical rollout.
The runtime calibration is deterministic algebra over the existing small own
contract/geometry and two named30-joint witnesses. No RNG is used.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import torch


def bind_own_leg_targets(wheel_geometry,contract,hip_anchors,device):
    path=Path(__file__).with_name('OWN_JUMP130_LEG_GEOMETRY_WITNESSES.json')
    doc=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    if doc['schema']!='mod4.jump130.own_static_leg_geometry_witnesses.v1':
        raise ValueError('130 own static witness schema')
    names=tuple(doc['joint_names']);actual=tuple(wheel_geometry.names)
    if len(names)!=30 or set(names)!=set(actual):raise ValueError('130 named30 witness')
    permutation=[names.index(n) for n in actual]
    results={};proof={}
    for role in ('tuck','extension'):
        row=doc[role]['row']
        if row['status']!='STATIC_KINEMATIC_FIT':raise ValueError('Rejected own static row cannot be selected')
        q=torch.tensor(row['joint_pos30'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)[permutation][None]
        for i,name in enumerate(actual):
            limits=contract['joints'][name]['limits_si']
            if limits is not None and not bool(((q[:,i]>=limits[0]-1e-6)&(q[:,i]<=limits[1]+1e-6)).all()):
                raise ValueError('Own static length witness violates imported joint limit '+name)
        zero=torch.zeros(1,3,device=device);rotation=torch.eye(3,device=device)[None]
        state=(zero,rotation,zero,zero,q,torch.zeros_like(q),torch.zeros(1,54,device=device))
        g=wheel_geometry(state)
        bodies=wheel_geometry.body_names
        wheel_ids=[bodies.index(x) for x in ('left_wheel','right_wheel')]
        targets=q.new_tensor(row['target_wheel_local_m'])
        # The original saved IK used wheel BODY origins, not mesh-centre means.
        fit=(g['body_position'][0,wheel_ids,:]-targets).norm(dim=-1).amax()
        if not bool(fit<=.001):raise ValueError('130 named own FK does not reproduce saved static wheel fit')
        errors=[]
        for closure in contract['closures'].values():
            a,b=[bodies.index(closure[k]) for k in ('body0','body1')]
            x=g['body_position'][0,a]+g['body_rotation'][0,a]@q.new_tensor(closure['anchor0_m'])
            y=g['body_position'][0,b]+g['body_rotation'][0,b]@q.new_tensor(closure['anchor1_m'])
            errors.append((x-y).norm())
        closure_error=torch.stack(errors).amax()
        if not bool(closure_error<=.002):raise ValueError('130 own static witness exceeds existing2mm closure limit')
        local=g['centre'][0]-hip_anchors
        lengths=local[:,[0,2]].norm(dim=-1)
        if not bool(torch.isfinite(lengths).all()):raise ValueError('Nonfinite own FK target length')
        results[role]=lengths
        proof[role]=dict(original_row_index=doc[role]['original_row_index'],
            length2_m=lengths.detach().cpu().tolist(),wheel_body_fit_max_m=float(fit),
            closure_max_m=float(closure_error),static_pose_only_not_dynamic_reachability=True)
    if not bool((results['extension']>results['tuck']).all()):
        raise ValueError('130 own witnesses do not define longer extension than tuck')
    return results,dict(schema=doc['schema'],source_sha256=doc['source_sha256'],
        source_contract_sha256=doc['own_import_contract_sha256'],poses=proof,
        foreign_dot31_dot16_not_selected=True,new_physics_or_IK_execution=False)

===== FULL UTF8 C:\Users\lulu\Desktop\claude\mjcf\MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021\MOD4_H1_OVERNIGHT_FUDAN_SUPERMIMIC_REPAIR_GOAL_20261027\native130_Fudan_jump_takeoff_own_geometry_reward_source_v1\source\jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py SEGMENT 0 =====
"""Source130: pinned Fudan JUMP formulas adapted to own MOD4 step geometry.

The historical filename is an explicit overlay. This is not source128.
Original126 success/process memory is independent of dense jump encouragement.
No action, force, reset, trajectory controller or physical success override.
"""
import torch
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,smooth01

UPSTREAM_COMMIT='8204e853dfd2ed06d85a322e1a998c3d20a3be2c'


def own_wheel_height_score(bottom2,step_height=.27):
    """Fudan exp(-6*abs(height-target)), in actual wheel-bottom coordinates.

    Remove its floor offset and normalize BEFORE scaling to the original
    per-term2.5 cap. Thus zero at floor and strictly increasing to270mm;
    the original6*exp then clip would saturate well BEFORE its target.
    """
    floor=bottom2.new_tensor(-6*step_height).exp()
    each=((torch.exp(-6*(bottom2-step_height).abs())-floor)/(1-floor)).clamp(0,1)
    return each.amin(-1)


def evaluate(m,mode,legacy_nonrequest,effort_quality,*,launched,crossed,closure_m,public_age,p=CommandReward()):
    P=mode['top_support'];v=m['world_velocity3'];active=m['request_active']
    landing=P*crossed.to(P.dtype)
    desired_x=mode['base_command_vx']*(1-landing)
    error=(v[...,0]-desired_x).square()+v[...,1].square()
    # Original tracking and enhanced kernels/weights. Limit the enhanced cost
    # to its matching positive tracker: a bad command cannot make total reward
    # negative and reward early death. This is a declared safety adaptation.
    tracking=2*torch.exp(-error/.25)
    enhanced=torch.maximum(2*(torch.exp(-error/2.5)-1),-tracking)
    heading=(1-m['heading_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)
    alignment=torch.exp(-heading/(1-.9800665778412416)-m['root_y'].square()/.3**2)
    leading_front=m['wheel_high2'][...,0].amax(-1)
    before_edge=smooth01((p.edge_x-leading_front)/.04)
    bottom=m['wheel_bottom2'];minimum=bottom.amin(-1)
    H=own_wheel_height_score(bottom,p.step_height)
    clearance=(minimum/p.step_height).clamp(0,1)
    # Current observable geometry + two-control wheel-force history surrogate.
    # NO ready prerequisite, no16-callback prerequisite, no vx/near prerequisite.
    # This permits dense takeoff learning in the actual observed ready=0 branch.
    # It never changes the stricter original physical/process qualification.
    air=smooth01(minimum/.005)*smooth01(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']/.005)*m['unloaded_both']
    stage=torch.maximum(air,launched.to(v.dtype))
    # Preserve height/tuck credit through a qualified partial-footprint landing.
    # Otherwise first real top contact would erase the air terms before P rises.
    region=m['task_lane_and_far_bound'].to(v.dtype)
    jump=(1-P)*region
    upward=v[...,2].clamp(0,2.4)  # original.15*vz+, bounded at inherited engineering reference
    own_lift=torch.minimum(m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'],m['wheel_centre_velocity2']).amin(-1)
    conservative_up=torch.minimum(v[...,2],own_lift).clamp_min(0)
    line_z=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*air*(1-clearance)
    length=m['virtual_leg_length2']
    extend=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_extension_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    tuck=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_tuck_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    # Preserve Fudan's support + root-vz>.15 takeoff definition, with measured
    # support history as an explicitly imperfect proxy. No mandatory crouch.
    takeoff=m['supported_any']*(v[...,2]>.15).to(v.dtype)*before_edge
    quiet=torch.exp(-v.square().sum(-1)/.03**2-m['world_angular_velocity3'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -(1-m['upright_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)/(1-.984807753012208)
        -heading/(1-.9800665778412416))
    dt=.01*effort_quality
    quality=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()*(public_age>=10).to(v.dtype)
    terms=dict(fudan_tracking_lin_vel=dt*tracking*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_lin_vel_enhance=dt*enhanced*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_ang_vel=dt*torch.exp(-m['body_yaw_velocity'].square()/.25)*active,
        fudan_encourage_jump_upward=dt*.15*upward*jump*active,
        fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry=dt*.5*extend*takeoff*jump*active,
        fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent=dt*line_z*jump*active,
        fudan_flight_real_clearance=dt*.15*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        fudan_base_height_flight_own_wheel270=dt*2.5*H*stage*jump*active,
        fudan_leg_tuck_own_geometry=dt*1.7*tuck*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        supported_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*12*landing*active,
        quiet_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*4*landing*quiet*active,
        closure_compliance_cost=torch.zeros_like(dt),
        original_nonrequest_recovery=(1-active)*legacy_nonrequest)
    positive=sum(value for name,value in terms.items() if name not in ('closure_compliance_cost','original_nonrequest_recovery'))
    terms['closure_compliance_cost']=-.5*quality*positive
    total=sum(terms.values())
    if not bool(torch.isfinite(total).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite130 reward')
    return dict(total=total,selected_terms=terms,command_vx=desired_x,
        command_wheel_bottom_height=.27,command_wheel_bottom_upward_velocity=torch.full_like(H,2.4),
        planning_top_support=P,qualified_cross=crossed,before_edge_gate=before_edge,
        both_wheel_height_score=H,closure_quality_cost=quality,
        dense_air_geometry_history_surrogate=air,dense_stage_with_qualified_landing_bridge=stage,
        true_wheel_clearance_fraction=clearance,conservative_upward_velocity=conservative_up,
        own_extension_length2=m['own_extension_length2'],own_tuck_length2=m['own_tuck_length2'],
        process_ready=m['process_ready'],dense_incentives_do_not_require_ready=True,
        process126_unchanged=True,upstream_commit=UPSTREAM_COMMIT,
        original_stateful_airtime_accumulator_not_selected=True,
        source_only_not_runtime_admitted=True,not_original_Fudan_two_policy_reward=True)


def combine_closure_risk(old_soft_done,closure_m,public_age):
    """Continuous engineering constraint-risk cost, not calibrated failure probability.

    The physical2mm guard is unchanged. Predicted1.2..1.8mm now has nonzero
    cost even though the WM underestimates the saved actual failure. This does
    not claim the underlying WM closure prediction has been repaired.
    """
    risk=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()
    risk=torch.where(public_age>=10,risk,torch.zeros_like(risk))
    per_step=-torch.expm1(torch.log1p(-risk.clamp_max(1-1e-6))/32.)
    combined=1-(1-old_soft_done)*(1-per_step)
    return torch.where((public_age>=10)&(closure_m>.002),torch.ones_like(combined),combined)

"""Source130: selected Fudan JUMP families with own geometric targets.
Original recovery is unchanged off-request; old foreign-length JUMP values are
labelled diagnostics, while selected_optimization_terms is the actual objective.
"""
import torch
from legacy_fudan111_reward_diagnostics_v1 import (
    BoundOwnStepReward,UPSTREAM_COMMIT,NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS,
    _weighted,instantaneous_jump_terms,instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms,JUMP_WEIGHTS,RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,
    contact_and_step_modes as legacy_modes)
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,modes as command_modes
from jump_process_command_reward122_v1 import evaluate
PARAMETERS=CommandReward()

def contact_and_step_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p):
    assert p.step_top_z_m==PARAMETERS.step_height and p.step_edge_x_m==PARAMETERS.edge_x and p.platform_end_x_m==PARAMETERS.far_x
    old=legacy_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p)
    new=command_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,PARAMETERS)
    old.update(new,top_support=new['top_support'],jump=new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),recovery=1-new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),
        command_forward_velocity=new['base_command_vx']*(1-new['top_support']),wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],
        command_reward116=True,legacy_flight_is_diagnostic_only=True)
    return old

def combined_reward(m,p,mode):
    j,jparts=_weighted(instantaneous_jump_terms(m,p),JUMP_WEIGHTS,2.5,p.control_dt_s)
    r,rparts=_weighted(instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms(m,p),RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,1.,p.control_dt_s)
    # PINNED JUMP penalty weights, as bounded attenuation to prevent early-death
    # escape from negative request rewards. No unavailable term is forged zero.
    effort_names=('action_rate','torques','nominal_state','orientation','ang_vel_xy','pen_theta_no0')
    penalty=sum(jparts[k] for k in effort_names)/p.control_dt_s
    assert bool((penalty<=1e-6).all())
    effort=torch.exp(.05*penalty.clamp_min(-10.))
    data=dict(wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],world_velocity3=torch.cat((m['task_planar_velocity'],m['world_vertical_velocity'][...,None]),-1),
        world_angular_velocity3=m['world_angular_velocity'],request_active=m['request_active'],heading_cos=m['heading_cos'],upright_cos=m['upright_cos'],root_y=m['root_lateral_position'])
    data.update(wheel_high2=m['wheel_high2'],wheel_bottom_velocity2=m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'],
        wheel_centre_velocity2=m['wheel_centre_velocity2'],body_yaw_velocity=m['body_yaw_velocity'],
        virtual_leg_length2=m['virtual_leg_length'],own_extension_length2=m['own_extension_length2'],
        own_tuck_length2=m['own_tuck_length2'],nonwheel_clearance_m=m['nonwheel_clearance_m'],
        unloaded_both=m['unloaded_both'],supported_any=m['supported_any'],
        task_lane_and_far_bound=m['task_lane_and_far_bound'],process_ready=m['process_ready'])
    out=evaluate(data,mode,r,effort,launched=m['process_launched'],crossed=m['process_crossed'],closure_m=m['closure_m'],public_age=m['public_age'],p=PARAMETERS)
    assert bool(((out['total']>=-1e-7)|(m['request_active']==0)).all())
    return dict(total=out['total'],selected_optimization_terms=out['selected_terms'],
        process_command122=out,command_tracking116=dict(command_vx=out['command_vx'],target_each_wheel_bottom_m=PARAMETERS.step_height,
            actual_wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],both_wheel_height_score=out['both_wheel_height_score'],

    # yaw_mat(R).T @ R has an identically zero (1,0) entry, barring
    # FP32 roundoff. In the existing 6D column encoding this is feature3
    # after the height. Replace only that redundant zero with .01*yaw/pi.
    # .01 is the inherited RunningNorm eps, giving a bounded <=1 signal
    # even when the old zero slot's variance/count is frozen. Goal/tilt,
    # all weights, all norm tensors and all three optimizers stay inherited.
    # Wrapped yaw has a declared representation seam at +/-pi; it is not
    # an independent RNG stream, a model reset, or a sine-only heading alias.
    @staticmethod
    def _with_task_heading(value,state):
        R=state[1]
        yaw=torch.atan2(R[...,1,0],R[...,0,0])
        return torch.cat((value[...,:3],(.01*yaw/math.pi).unsqueeze(-1),value[...,4:]),-1)

        request=((age>=200)&(age<2000)).to(features.dtype)

    def closure_error(self,g):

        b=p[...,self.closure_i1,:]+(R[...,self.closure_i1,:,:]@self.closure_a1[...,None]).squeeze(-1)

    def advance_reward_frame(self,state,next_state,frames):

    def objective_terms(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):

    def reward(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):

        if torch.is_inference_mode_enabled():self.last_reward_components=result

    def soft_termination(self,state,frames):
        # Physical low_base is disabled at -1m; do not invent a .12m hard failure.
        # Declare the sigmoid as a 32-control horizon risk (engineering surrogate),
        # not the same full risk repeated every 10ms. Exact physical tilt/timeout
        # masks remain certain here; all real physics termination functions stay unchanged.
        cos_tilt=state[1][...,2,2]
        horizon_risk=torch.sigmoid((math.cos(.75)-cos_tilt)/.04)

        return combine_closure_risk(original,self.closure_error(self.wheel_geometry(state)),decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age'])

             history_free=None,body_height=.4):

    def path(name):
        rows=[];launch_v=math.sqrt(2*9.81*.35)
        touch=(launch_v+math.sqrt(launch_v**2-2*9.81*.27))/9.81

            elif name=='compression_drive_jump_top':
                if i<12:
                    row.update(root_vz=-.2 if i<4 else 2.4*(i-4)/7,leg=tuck+(ext-tuck)*max(0.,(i-4)/7))
                else:

                        row.update(height=launch_v*t-.5*9.81*t*t,root_vz=vz,wheel_vz=vz,force=0.,
                            vx=min(.5,5*t),front=.25+dx,leg=ext+(tuck-ext)*min(t/.12,1.))

            elif name!='static_stand':raise ValueError(name)
            rows.append(row)
        return rows

    def discounted(seq,horizon,repeat=False):
        extseq=(seq*((horizon+len(seq)-1)//len(seq)))[:horizon] if repeat else (seq+[seq[-1]]*max(0,horizon-len(seq)))[:horizon]
